;(function(win, doc, style, timeout) {
  var STYLE_ID = 'at-body-style';

  function getParent() {
    return doc.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
  }

  function addStyle(parent, id, def) {
    if (!parent) {
      return;
    }

    var style = doc.createElement('style');
    style.id = id;
    style.innerHTML = def;
    parent.appendChild(style);
  }

  function removeStyle(parent, id) {
    if (!parent) {
      return;
    }

    var style = doc.getElementById(id);

    if (!style) {
      return;
    }

    parent.removeChild(style);
  }

  addStyle(getParent(), STYLE_ID, style);
  setTimeout(function() {
    removeStyle(getParent(), STYLE_ID);
  }, timeout);
}(window, document, "body {opacity: 0 !important}", 3000));
</script>

    <div class="container responsivegrid cmp-top-spacing-48 cmp-bottom-spacing-84 aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">

    
    
    
    <div id="container-384c8521c7" class="cmp-container cmp-container--view">
        <div class="cmp-container-flex">
            


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="richtext text cmp-bottom-spacing-24 aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div data-icon-aria-label=" (opens in a new tab)" id="richtext-6c56a2aa8f" class="cmp-text rich-text-container">
    <div class="rich-text-wrapper">
        <h2 class="richtext--element-font"><span class="color brand_color"><span class="variant headlineLarge"><b><span class="color brand_color"><span class="color brand_color"></span></span></b><span class="color brand_color"><span class="color brand_color"><span class="variant headlineLarge"><b>Frequently asked questions</b></span></span></span><b></b></span></span></h2>
 
        <!-- Used unsafe context as temporary fix and create adobe support ticket to
        figure out why html context adding extra ul and li elements -->
    </div>
</div>

    





    

</div>
<div class="accordion aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">


    
</div>
<div class="accordion aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div>
    <dig-accordion data-cmp-multiple-expansion>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item0">
                Does Thomson Reuters or its third parties retain user content or prompts to train their GenAI-based products or underlying large-language models (LLM)?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item0">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>No. Your user content and prompts are not used to train or improve CoCounsel and associated products or LLMs, used in Thomson Reuters or third-party output, or stored by OpenAI or Google.<br />
<br />
Thomson Reuters AI third-party partners, such as OpenAI and Google, are contractually prohibited from using any customer data to train their models. Additionally, where applicable, we have established systemic controls to turn off third parties&#39; abuse-monitoring solutions to ensure there is no human access to Thomson Reuters customers&#39; data or inclusion of that data in third-party models.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item1">
                How long does Thomson Reuters retain user content and prompts?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item1">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Retention of user content and prompts is customized to fit customer needs and set up at the organization account level with the support of a Thomson Reuters client manager. We then delete user content and prompts in accordance with the customer&#39;s retention policy. Users can delete their content and prompts in the product at any time. </p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item2">
                How does Thomson Reuters protect user privacy and data?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item2">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>All GenAI LLM requests originating from Thomson Reuters are processed under the identity &#34;Thomson Reuters,&#34; never identifying the Thomson Reuters customer who made the request. We secure all data in transit via TLS 1.2, and symmetric encryption (AES-256) protects data at rest. All interactions with GenAI LLM providers use zero-retention API calls.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item3">
                Where are user content and user prompts stored when retained?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item3">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p><p>Systematic procedures and hosting are in the U.S. We process all interactions with OpenAI GPT and Google Gemini in the U.S. We host backup data in separate availability time zones in the U.S.*</p>
<p>*Region-based hosting is available in the UK, Australia, and Canada.</p>
</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item4">
                What guardrails are in place to prevent bias in Thomson Reuters content?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item4">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>We have developed and implemented prompting &#34;guardrails,&#34; ensuring our GenAI-based products will only produce responses from content drawn from a clearly defined and limited set of verified information, such as our own content databases. This practice restricts the large language model&#39;s ability to produce potentially objectionable content broadly sourced from the internet.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item5">
                What is Fiduciary‑Grade AI?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item5">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Fiduciary‑Grade AI is the Thomson Reuters standard for how AI should work in high-stakes professions. It is AI designed for professionals with duties of care and regulatory oversight — drawing on authoritative, domain-specific content; protected by rigorous privacy and security safeguards; shaped by subject-matter experts; and designed to produce transparent outputs that can be verified.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item6">
                How is Fiduciary‑Grade AI different from general-purpose AI?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item6">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>General-purpose AI is useful for broad productivity tasks. Fiduciary‑Grade AI is designed for professional work where outputs may influence legal judgments, financial disclosures, regulatory filings, compliance decisions, or client advice. It emphasizes authoritative sources, privacy, expert involvement, and verifiable outputs.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item7">
                Does Fiduciary‑Grade AI replace professional judgment?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_container_1689392523_accordion_547511501_accordionItems_item7">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>No. Fiduciary‑Grade AI is designed to support human judgment, not replace it. Professionals remain responsible for the advice they give, the decisions they make, and the work they sign off on.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
    </dig-accordion>
</div>

    
</div>

    
</div>

        </div>
    </div>

    
</div>

    
</div>

        </div>
    </div>

    
</div>

                <script type="text/javascript">
// Hide OneTrust banner if wcmmode=disabled is present in URL or in PROD preview domain
if (window.location.search.includes('wcmmode=disabled') || window.location.host.includes('preview-prod-cs.ewp.thomsonreuters.com')) {
    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.textContent = '[id*="onetrust"],[class*="onetrust"],[id*="ot-"],[class*="ot-"]{display:none!important}';
    document.head.appendChild(style);
}

// function to pull cookie value
function getCookie(name) {
    var value = "; " + document.cookie;
    var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
    if (parts.length == 2) return parts.pop().split(";").shift();
}
function OptanonWrapper() { 
        setTimeout(() => {
            _satellite.pageBottom();
        }, 1000);
    var OABCcookieName = "OptanonConsent";
    var bannerAcceptBtn = document.getElementById("onetrust-accept-btn-handler");
    var pcAllowAllBtn = document.getElementById("accept-recommended-btn-handler");
    var pcSaveBtn = document.getElementsByClassName("save-preference-btn-handler onetrust-close-btn-handler")[0];
    var OABCcookie = getCookie(OABCcookieName);
        
   
    // IF logic needed here because ot-banner-sdk DIV is not injected on page loads if banner is not exposed
    if (!OABCcookie && bannerAcceptBtn) {
        bannerAcceptBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    }
    if (pcAllowAllBtn)
        pcAllowAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    if(pcSaveBtn){
        pcSaveBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            setTimeout(()=> {
            location.reload();
        }, 1000) //quick timeout so that the consent receipt can be sent and the cookie can be updated
        });
    }
} </script>
